Directory Structure

Collapsible directory tree showing file and symbol counts. Directories with LLM summaries show them inline.

java/5 files · 20 symbols
This Java directory contains utilities for processing BMDExpress 3 benchmark dose (BMD) analysis data, including exporters for genomics results, category analyses, and integrated projects in JSON/TSV formats. It also includes a statistical prefiltering tool that performs Williams trend tests and Dunnett's comparisons on dose-response data using the same libraries as BMDExpress itself. Together, these tools enable extracting, integrating, and analyzing BMD results across multiple project files and experimental conditions.
output/1 files · 1 symbols
The `output` directory contains a Python script that generates formatted Word documents describing a toxicogenomics interpretation pipeline. This pipeline processes gene expression data through multiple analysis stages to produce comprehensive, literature-grounded reports for scientific research or clinical applications.
pdf_text/3 files · 51 symbols
A lightweight, zero-dependency PDF parsing library that extracts semantic text from PDFs by resolving object graphs, decoding compressed streams, and applying structure tags from marked-content operators. It includes comprehensive support for ToUnicode CMaps, font encodings, and table detection, plus a lossless codec that can decompose PDFs into labeled byte spans and reconstruct them byte-perfectly.
tests/25 files · 353 symbols
The tests directory contains the test suite for the project with pytest configuration and fixtures. conftest.py provides isolated testing infrastructure including state management (patched filesystem), a FastAPI test client, fixture data, and mocks for external dependencies like Java subprocesses, HTTP requests, and the Anthropic API. This setup enables unit and integration testing of the codebase in a controlled environment.
e2e/4 files · 18 symbols
tests/e2e contains end-to-end tests for a data pipeline system using Playwright, validating the complete flow from session loading through validation, integration, processing, and approval. It provides shared testing fixtures, debugging utilities with screenshots and manual inspection checkpoints, and smoke tests using golden session data to verify pipeline correctness.
fixtures/1 files · 0 symbols
The tests/fixtures directory is a Python package that serves as a container for test fixtures and test data. Currently, it contains only an empty `__init__.py` file, making it available as an importable package for the test suite.
integration/6 files · 45 symbols
Integration tests for a FastAPI backend system that manages chemical/BMD data workflows, covering file upload endpoints, pool validation and integration for merging multiple data sources, session lifecycle management with version control, and processing integrated data with domain model validation across multiple platforms.
unit/12 files · 279 symbols
This directory contains a comprehensive unit test suite for a scientific data processing and reporting system, covering core domain logic like BMD project schema validation, document tree structure, footnote management, and data integration, as well as output generation modules for HTML and LaTeX export, partitioning and filtering, and session persistence. The tests verify functional correctness of utility functions, schema constraints, data transformations, and file I/O operations across the codebase.
